{\em cpuhp\+\_\+setup\+\_\+state\+\_\+cpuslocked -\/ Setup hotplug state callbacks with calling @startup callback from a \mbox{\hyperlink{include_2linux_2cpu_8h_a9e4e4f967d8f3bda7bc2c12783d1cd0a}{cpus\+\_\+read\+\_\+lock()}} held region @state\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} state for which the calls are installed

\begin{Indent}\textbf{ \+: Name of the callback.}\par
{\em cpuhp\+\_\+setup\+\_\+state\+\_\+multi -\/ Add callbacks for multi state @state\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} state for which the calls are installed

@startup\+: startup callback function or N\+U\+LL if not required @teardown\+: teardown callback function or N\+U\+LL if not required

Sets the internal multi\+\_\+instance flag and prepares a state to work as a multi instance callback. No callbacks are invoked at this point. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} callbacks are invoked once an instance for this state are registered via \mbox{\hyperlink{cpuhotplug_8h_ae78efee564d9ad1312e577ca9bf61ba7}{cpuhp\+\_\+state\+\_\+add\+\_\+instance()}} or \mbox{\hyperlink{cpuhotplug_8h_a021eae9f7a961994ce17a7d6119a1be7}{cpuhp\+\_\+state\+\_\+add\+\_\+instance\+\_\+nocalls()}} }\begin{DoxyCompactItemize}

{\footnotesize\ttfamily void \+\_\+\+\_\+cpuhp\+\_\+remove\+\_\+state\+\_\+cpuslocked (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{interfaceenum}{enum}} \mbox{\hyperlink{cpuhotplug_8h_ad381464bc72134a3b64727f737ff44ac}{cpuhp\+\_\+state}}}]{state,  }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{include_2linux_2types_8h_afaa87723b8417d40fcf45b7330261ef9}{bool}}}]{invoke }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

\+\_\+\+\_\+cpuhp\+\_\+remove\+\_\+state\+\_\+cpuslocked -\/ Remove the callbacks for an hotplug machine state @state\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} state to remove @invoke\+: If true, the teardown function is invoked for cpus where cpu state $>$= @state

\mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} caller needs to hold cpus read locked while calling this function. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} teardown callback is currently not allowed to fail. Think about module removal! Here is the call graph for this function\+:

cpuhp\+\_\+state\+\_\+add\+\_\+instance -\/ Add an instance for a state and invoke startup callback. @state\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} state for which the instance is installed @node\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} node for this individual state.
